<p class="para" style="margin-top: 0px; margin-bottom: 1.5rem; color: rgb(51, 51, 51); font-family: &#39;Fira Sans&#39;, &#39;Source Sans Pro&#39;, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; line-height: 24px; white-space: normal; background-color: rgb(242, 242, 242);">The &quot;Changeable&quot; column shows the modes determining when and where a directive may be set.
